WWA (1997–2019) Card Archive

Rage in the Cage VII - March 21, 2004

21 March 2004
Content noticeA period document, served unedited. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
What this is The full staged script of the World Wrestling Alliance’s Rage in the Cage VII pay-per-view (with SCWF crossover matches), covering its opening, commentary, backstage segments, cage matches, a Chamber from Hell match and the closing World Title main event, plus archive ’random fact’ and ’random memory’ recaps of earlier Rage in the Cage shows.
Slowly the camera pans through the streets and alleyways of Birmingham as the camera begins to move faster and faster through the streets to the Arena where suddenly the WWA logo flashes upon the screen as Unholy by Kiss begins to blast in the background and all hell breaks loose as the fans and pyro's explode around the arena and flames erupt up and down the stage. I was there through the ages The scene opens with Mark Harrington standing there with his arms crossed grinning as he stares straight forward. Chained slaves to their cages The WWA's former Heavyweight Champions flash upon the screen from previous Rage in the Cage events as one is seen at the very end and thats Grunge! I have seen you eat your own Leviathan, the green eyed Monster is staring at Deimos who is staring back at him with utter determination between the two as the US Title hangs in the background. I'm the cycle of pain Muppet Man stares at Tommy Starr as they both are looking rather determined to what is needed of them as they step forward nose to nose. Of a thousand year old reign Dave Harley and Mal Somers are staring each other down as Harley is holding the belt over his shoulder and tapping it as Mal Somers looks on with determination. I'm suicide and salvation Enigma is looking at Bridge and grins as he looks to the left at the Fiesta logo and stares at Bridge once more. The omen to nations Vik Ventressa and Denrol are staring each other down as scenes flash from their last few matches in the background as a shadowed figure looms in the background with his hands on his hips unmoving and unseen. That you worship on all fours Ian Tense, Venny LeTrav, Fathom and SEAL stand before the camera just looking forward nodding their heads as if they know something nobody else does. I'm the infection and famine Brad Polaris holds the TV Championship across his waist as he looks at Khaled who looks back and nods his head and steps forward looking Brad Polaris square in the eyes... That's knocking at your door Quietus is looking across the way at three men he will be facing as Chuckster, Liam Jenson, and Stephen Murphy just sneer back at him seemingly ready to go after him at a moments notice. That's why you're feeling so Fat Tony and Jimmy Valentine look at the camera as the Meltdown logo flashes behind them.. Unholy Oh, I was created by man, you know I'm.... Mikal and Chase look at the camera standing back to back as the Fiesta logo flashes behind them.. Unholy Yeah, yeah, yeah, yeah The EWA symbol is crushed by SCWF symbol as Joe Stock stands there with a grin and lowers his glasses looking above them as the camera zooms in to them.. I am the incubus Rocky Adams and Armaan stand nose to nose looking at each other with determination as the SCWF Title belt is held in Rocky Adams hands. I lay the egg in you Bonafide looks across the way at a shadowed man who has a t-shirt which is lighted up and reads Mr. E and Bonafide just points and clenches a fist... The worm that burrows Through your brain Joey K holds the Gen X strap and tips his hat towards Jimmi Zion and Matt Keith who both look at Joey K with anger and rage wanting to unleash on him right there. But you are the beast That calls me by my name Tracer Bullet looks at Nick Adams and grins and he crosses his arms and Adams looks back and grins a cocky grin and waits patiently.. You send your children to war To serve bastards and whores So now you know You created me On the day that you were born Matt Keith and Chance hold the WWA Tag Team Titles looking over at two mystery men behind a shaded screen and pat the titles motioning the two to bring it on and then look at each other disgusted... Unholy I was created by man, yeah I'm the Lord of the flies, you know I'm Unholy From the left hand of power comes the father of lies Unholy - Unholy! Matt Warr just looks at the camera with arms crossed and grins as he watches and waits for the nights actions to begin.. I lay you down to sleep Your soul to keep Better cross your heart before you die And now you know Know that you are mine Shaman, Chance, and Wes Sanders look upwards at the WWA World Heavyweight Championship shining down upon them through the light as they all drop their gaze and look at each other eye to eye with no emotion and they watch each other and turn to look at Grunge who stands off to the left a bit further back than they are standing... That's why you're feeling so Unholy, Unholy, Unholy - Unholy!! The camera pans around the fans and then circles the arena and then down to the announcing booth as the crowd is at a deafening roar as Fat Tony and Mikail shake hands and Chase and Jimmy do likewise and then look at the camera as the WWA Rage in the Cage logo pops up... BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOOOOOOOOOOOOOM! World Wrestling Alliance Presents... LIVE! From The National Exhibition Center Arena Birmingham, England Sunday, 21st March 2004 Jimmy: Live from the NEC Arena in Birmingham England we bring you!!! Chase: Rage in the Cage!! Fat Tony: Thats right Baby! Mikal: Shut up or i vill crush you! Jimmy and Fat Tony take their seats as Mikail and Chase take theirs nearby... Jimmy: I can hardly believe it. 7 years ago, Mark Harrington announced his intention to start a new wrestling promotion and have a Pay per View Spectacular to celebrateits early success. And here we are, 7 years later. They said it couldn't be done... Fat Tony: But we did it! I bet all them nay-sayers are eating their hats right now... Jimmy: We have such an awesome lineup for you tonight, it is unbelieveable. Fat Tony: Shame we have the russian loser ruining the evening for everyone... Mikal: Oy! Jimmy: From the Meltdown side of things its going to be an exciting night folks! Fat Tony: Thats right we have Tracer Bullet and Nick Adams squaring off to settle a personal grudge! Jimmy: In a 45 minute Chamber From Hell match! Fat Tony: Wooo! Jimmy: We have Leviathan and Deimos squaring off for the US Title! Fat Tony: The world will stop on that match! Jimmy: We also have Tommy Starr and Muppetman facing off in a match of up and coming stars! Fat Tony: How could we forget the legends battling it out with Vik Ventressa and Denrol facing each other to settle the score! Jimmy: The TV Title is on the line as Brad Polaris and Khaled duke it out to see who is the true champion! Fat Tony: Then we have Chuckster, Liam Jenson, and Stephen Murphy facing of against Fiesta's own Quietus! Jimmy: ..And in the Main Event we have two Meltdown stars in the Heavyweight Champion Grunge and WWA Legend Shaman representing Meltdown and trying to get the World title or defend it! The camera quickly switches to Mikail and Chase.. Chase: On the Fiesta side of the coin we have Mal Somers and Dave Harley facing off for the Intercontinental Title! Mikal: No gimmicks there Tovarsich! Chase: Matt Keith and Chance defend the WWA Tag Team titles against Warr's mystery men who could that be? Mikal: Ze mystery between Mr. E and Bonafide will be revealed very soon in tonights match! Chase: Thats right and we also have Joey K defending his Gen X Title against Jimmi Zion and Matt Keith two very hungry competitors for gold! Mikal: In ze Job vs Commish match we have Enigma vs Bridge! Chase: Should have long lasting consequences there! Mikal: Da that is to be guessed! Chase: ..And in the Main Event of the Night we have Chance and Wes Sanders joining Meltdowns two representatives to fight for the World Heavyweight Championship!! The camera suddenly flashes and jolts as the SCWF symbol flashes on the screen as Johnny Morehouse and Terry Valentine pop up on the screen.. Johnny: The SCWF will defend its World Heavyweight Title right here as well!! Terry: Yes thats right with Rocky Adams and Armaan battling out to see who is the man! Johny: Morehouse: Its great to back at Rage in the Cage! Terry: Its great to be back in England baby!! We cut back to Jimmy and Tony. Jimmy: Who invited those outcasts? Fat Tony: Yeah this is WWA turf, get lost! Before things get too heated, we cut backstage and see Ian Tense, sitting in his wheelchair with his fingers interlaced together on his chin. He is being wheeled down the corridor by one of his few remaining security guards Ian: Hurry up. I have business to attend to. Guard: Yessir. To XCorps locker room then? Ian: No. This is a more personal venture. Take me to my locker room. Guard: Of Course, sir. As the guard turns the corner, a massive figure blocks the hallway. Voice: My how the mighty have fallen. Ian shoots a glance at the figure Ian: Leviathan. Did you come to pay homage? Levi: looking at Ian's wheelchair Homage? To a shell? Really Ian, you must be kidding. Ian: A Shell? You just remember who made you what you are today. Without me, you'd still be fighting undercard matches against local jobbers. Lev: I seriously doubt it Ian. Besides, you've got it turned around. Without me, you wouldn't have that lengthy title reign you so proudly cling to. But that was some time ago. Look at yourself now. You've become a burden to yourself. Ian jerks forward, ripping the wheelchair from his guard's grasp. He wheels towards his former teammate. Ian: Guard, leave us... Guard: Sir? Ian: You heard me. The guard hesitantly leaves. Ian stares up at the forboding figure Ian: A Burden? Ian's face vecomes entrenched in a nearly uncontrollable rage Ian: A *beep*ing burden!? That's all you can say to the man that helped make you?! Ian starts to try to stand up, but his injuries are still tender, making his arms and legs shake under the unsuspecting pressure of trying to stand Levi: Ian... Leviathan puts one of his massive hands on Ian's shoulders and shoves him backwards into the wheelchair. Levi: Don't embarrass yourself. Levi smirks and pushes the chair to one side and strides past. Ian: You just made one helluva *beep*ing mistake, Thomas!!! You just *beep*ing made the biggest mistake of your life!!!! Levi: I'll remember you said that, Ian. Ian pounds the arm rests of his wheelchair a couple of times in undultered fury. Ian: GUARD!!! The guard pops back from around the corner. Ian: Get me out of here NOW! The two disappear down the corridor and we head back to the commentators. Well a commentator and Mikal. Chase: Well folks, the time has come for the first match of the evening, and what a match it will be. Mikal: Vhy am I here? Vhat is the vorld comink to? Chase: This match has been building for months and months. Dave Harley versus Mal Somers for the highly prized Intercontinental Title! Mikal: In a “No Gimmick” Gimmick match. Vhat da hell?! The house lights dim and the arena falls into blackness. The opening chords of “Can I Play With Madness” by Iron Maiden reverberate over the P.A. and the crowd begins to roar with cheers. Give me the sense to wonder To wonder if I'm free Give me a sense of wonder To know I can be me As the song kicks in, the stage area begins to glow with ultraviolet light, and a lone figure steps from the darkness into the eerie light. The crowd cheers as Mal Somers stands at the top of the ramp, shaking his head from side to side, adjusting the tape on his wrists, as the ultraviolet light gets brighter and brighter. Chase: And here comes the challenger, Mal Somers, and what a reaction by the crowd! Mikal: Oh dis sucks. Chase: He has been doubting his abilities but the recent polls show a slight majority of fans think Mal can pull it off! Mikal: Just like Bum Boozler. Chase: Whatever the result, this is going to be one heck of a match. The cage is hanging high, and will be lowered after this match. There are no gimmicks involved! The scene changes as two massive explosions of white pyro occur on either side of the ramp and Somers storms down towards the ring, through the smoke as the house lights come back on and words fill the screen over the highlights on Mal’s career this far. X - CORP - MAL SOMERS Give me the strength to hold my head up Spit back in their face Don't need no key to unlock this door Gonna break down the walls Break out of this bad place…. Can I play with Madness! Somers looks focused on the ring and determined, but still manages to slap hands with some loyal X-Corp fans on his way down the ramp, wearing his usual black wrestling trunks and “Laying the CRAXXT Down” T-Shirt. Gary Trudeau: Ladies and Gentlemen, this match is scheduled for one fall and is for the Intercontinental Title. First, making his way to the ring, weighing 250 pounds and hailing from Long Island New York….Mal Sommmmmeeeeeeeeeeerrrrrrrsssssssssss!!! Somers rolls in under the ropes, and ascends the nearest turnbuckles, holding his arms up high as the crowd cheers. He removes his T-Shirt and throws it out to the crowd before jumping down to the mat and waiting for his opponent. Chase: Get ready for an X-corp battle. Mikal: I hope dey kill each other. Chase: That could well happen. And there is Miss Wise sitting in the front row, cheering for Mal. Mikal: Vell dat is one. The camera shows Miss Wise, looking great in her tailored business suit and brown hair tied up in a bun ala Miss Hancock. She waves at the camera as… Mikal: My toes are tingling. Chase: Excitement? Mikal: No soda. Suddenly all the lights in the arena go out and the stadium is eerily pitch black. The crowd roars and then quietens down in anticipation of what will happen next. The opening chords of 'Superstar II' by Saliva blast over the PA Been floating around up here on Cloud 11 I did my best to make it to 27 Open up my eyes and I can see the glory Now I'm alive and I'm gonna tell my story The pyro explodes in a stream of light and the words "EXTREME CORPORATION – DAVE HARLEY" form in blood on the screen and then trickle away... Now I'm a superstar in the making. I ain't f**king around and there ain't no mistaking. I never ask for something worth the taking. Cause I'm a superstar, baby. The entranceway fills with smoke and strobe lighting illuminates the silhouette of The Extreme Machine! Harley dances out onto the stage as the crowd erupts. No crying-a$$ b*tching about my wife or girlfriend. Cause in my life I can't have either one. No crying-a$$ b*tching about my evil parents. They did their best to raise their only son. Gary Trudeau: Ladies and Gentlemen, making his way to the ring. The WWA Intercontinental Champion, ‘The Extreme Machine’ Daaaaaaveeeee Haaaaaaaarrrrllllleeeeeeeeeeyy!!! Dave steps out through the smoke. Chase: It’s been a long time in the making, but tonight we finally see Dave Harley v Mal Somers…right now! Mikal: Mikal bored already. Is Main Event yet? He makes his way down to ringside, slapping hands with the loyal X Corp fans on the way. He is wearing the brand new ‘HARLEY 435’ T-Shirt. He slides in to the ring and poses on the turnbuckle with his Intercontinental Title. Chase: This is it Rage in the Cage about to kick off! Two friends battling for gold! Mikal: Is nothing Mikal always fights comrades. Chase: Yes, but Mal and Dave aren’t stinking with Vodka. Mikal: Bah! GIRLS! Dave extends his hand to his friend as the crowd cheer both men on. Chase: Dave wants to start this match off with a handshake. Mikal: Sissy X Corp girls… Chase: Don’t start. Mal wearily extends his hand and shakes that of his partner. But Dave then quickly turns the handshake into an Irish Whip. Mal bounces off the opposite ropes and Dave ducks down to hit a monkey flip. But Mal sees it coming and leapfrogs over his partner, stopping short of the opposite ropes. Chase: Dave takes Mal by surprise in the opening moments! Mikal: giggles Chase: Oh grow up Mikal! Somers and Harley lock up, and jockey for position, Harley’s strength winning out with a headlock. Mikal: All over!! All over!! Chase: Shut up. Somers reverses into a headlock of his own and backs into the ropes. Harley pushes him off and Somers runs the ropes. Chase: Clothesline by Harley, ducked by Somers who is gaining momentum. Somers rebounds with a flying forearm, which rattles Harley if nothing else. Somers goes for a DDT, but Harley wriggles free and the two lockup. Harley wins out with a facelock, dropping to one knee to lock it in tight. Chase: They have gelled as a team before. Mikal: Like mamas potato and vodka soup. After a few moments, Somers shifts position and takes Harley down to the mat and the crowd applauds. Somers lets go, and awaits for Harley to rise. The two circle each other to begin again. Chase: These two know each other inside and out. Mikal: giggles again Really! Chase: Look sock puppets! Mikal: ARRRRGGGHHH! As Chase makes Mikal relieve his fear of the woolly foot covering, Mal whips Dave into the ropes. But Harley baseball slides to the apron, coming back up on the other side with a big smile on his face. Mal smiles back and nods in approval. Chase: Mal looks impressed by Dave’s moves. Mikal: Dey should be fighting! Chase: But it’s not often you see a guy Harley’s size do stuff like that! Mal runs at Harley, but Dave shoulder barges him through the middle rope and sunset flips over his partner, taking him down for a pin. 1! 2! Chase: Kickout by Mal. I don’t think he was expecting that either! Mikal: Iz me or Somers very gullible? Chase: Mal Somers is a gifted athlete. Mikal: Well he is gifting this match to Harley. Mal pushes Dave off and quickly rolls to the outside. He asks for Dave to back off, which he does and smiles an his fellow X-corpian. He takes a few steps backward and waits for Somers to get to his foot on th apron steading himself to get back in. As he does, he runs over and connects wit a knee to the face. Chase: What a knee by Harley! He pulls Somers up by the hair and goes to whip him in to the guardrail, but Mal reverses it, sending Dave crashing into the barrier where several fans start slapping him. Chase: Dave reversed into the barrier. Mikal: Zee fans are hitting that pillock Harley! Chase: Pillock? Mikal: Got it from Mr Warr. Chase: Not the only thing you can catch off Warr. Mal holds his face and smiles as the fans enjoy patting the WWA’s resident ape. Mal charges and swings a vicious clothesline which Dave ducks and dumps Mal over the railing. Sensing the occasion Dave leaps onto the railing and beats his chest. Chase: The Mighty Pong roars! Mikal: Ze will be whimpering now, Chase: Oh ze will be. Damn you now I am speaking like you. Mikal: Vhat I hear nothing. Mal grabs a foot of Dave and he lands on the barricade, well like an instant Eunuch maker. Mal stands holding his back. And rolls the castrated monkey back towards the apron. Mal rolls Dave back into the ring. Mikal: That is how Russians desex their cats. Chase: Oh come on! Mikal: Well how the Russians dog lovers do. As Mikal enjoys his own joke, Mal whips Dave into the corner with a big thud. THUD! Chase: That was a big thud. Mikal: Agree. Told you it was big. Mal has Dave backed in to a corner. Dave shakes his head and puts up his hands in a ‘T’ shape. Chase: What’s this? Harley calling for a time out?! Mal looks puzzled, but Dave calls a ring attendant over who hands him two cans. He gives one to Mal and both men take a swig. Chase: Is that Dicktwerp?! Mikal: Not dat again! They both toss the half empty [or half full?] cans into the crowd as Mal resumes his beatdown on Harley. Chase: Seriously is there ever a good time for Dicktwerp. Mikal: Even Mikal can’t answer that. Mal sends Dave into the ropes. Chase: Harley would have been hurt if this was a cage match. Mikal: And dropping on a barricade tickles. Mal bounces off the ropes, but Dave takes him down with a drop toehold. He then jumps to his feet and grabs the ankle of Mal and applies an ankle lock. Mal winces with pain. Chase: Harley pulling out an ankle lock. Been a while since we’ve seen Dave unleash a strictly technical arsenal. Mikal: Harley had an arsenal? Dave releases the lock, but keeps hold of Mal’s leg. He lifts it high in the air and slams it down knee first to the mat. Mal lets out a howl of agony. Chase: That’s gotta hurt! Mal has recently aggravated a recurring knee injury and Dave is exploiting it. Mikal: Mikal bored. As Mal struggles to get up, Dave drags him further in to the ring and drops down, placing Mal’s leg in a scissor lock position and applying pressure. Mal squirms on his stomach. Chase: Wow! A reverse crucifix kneebar by Harley! He’s really going for Mal’s big weakness isn’t he? Mikal: Weakness, Somers ability is his weakness. Chase: Mal may make you eat them words. Mikal: How they are words? Chase: You actually made sense for a change. Mal Struggles and manages to reach the ropes. Dave releases the pressure on his friend and senses another opportunity. Chase: Harley is going up! Harley makes to ascend the turnbuckle, but Somers grabs him from behind and pulls him back down. Dave spins around and unleashes a series of fists on Somers. Chase: Harley with those big right hands on Somers As Mal staggers backwards out of the corner, Dave jumps up on to the second rope. He raises his elbow and slaps it as the crowd cheer him on. Somers shakes off the cobwebs, but as he charges, Dave leaps from the top. He catches Mal with a huge bionic elbow that knocks him off his feet as the crowd go crazy. Chase: Harley is going for a pin! 1! 2! Chase: Somers kicks out! Mikal: Vhen vill it end? Dave slaps the mat, but smiles. Chase: Harley knows he needs more than that to beat his X-corp friend. Somers and Harley lock up and Harley whips Somers into the ropes, ducking for a back body drop. Somers slows up, hooks Harley’s arms and picks him up in a double underhook, then puts him back down with a backbreaker. Somers gets to his feet and hits a legdrop across the midsection of his opponent. Chase: Somers helping Dave to his feet. Somers puts Dave’s arm over his shoulder and hooks in an abdominal stretch. Harley winces in pain as Somers torques in the hold. Chase: We all know what is coming next. Mikal: Dave beats Mal off? Chase: No, Mal hits a pumphandle backbreaker. Not this time, as Harley powers out with a big hiptoss on Somers, ratting the mat. Harley holds on and drops to one knee, digging the other into Somers’ back as he hooks in a sleeper hold of sorts. Mikal: Old school baybee! Chase: Huh? Mikal: Mikal school very old. Built in 1534. Harley has the hold locked in tight and Somers begins to go purple in the face. The referee grabs his arm, and drops it once. Harley grins as Somers’ arm is lifted a second time and drops again. Chase: This could be it, and what a way to go. Somers arm is lifted a third time, and the referee lets go. Chase: Somers’ arm is up! There is fight in him yet! Mikal: Bah! Somers, using all his reserves, struggles and gets to his feet. The crowd spurring him on, Somers elbows Harley once, twice, three times, and Dave finally lets go. Somers shakes his head to clear the cobwebs, but it is too late as Harley sends Somers down to the mat with a thunderous clothesline. Dave collapses on one knee to get his breath. Chase: Both men are using a lot of energy. Mikal: Energy? They eat happy meals! Chase: X-corp claim it to be the food of champions. The wear and tear of this match is now clear in both men’s eyes. They stand staring at each other exhausted as the crowd cheer. Crowd: X CORP! X CORP! X CORP! Chase: Each man has to be thinking how the hell am I supposed to win this? Mikal: Mikal thinking hurry up and finish! Dave makes to tie up with Mal, but pulls away at the last second, kicking Mal in the gut and swiftly lifting him in Harley Bomb position. Chase: HARLEY B… Wait! No! Mal slides free and lands on his feet. He bounces off the ropes and fires back with a huge clothesline. Both men lay exhausted on the mat as the referee begins a ten count. Chase: A complete reversal of fortune there! Mikal: Vhere is Somers going? Chase: Harley is down for the count! Mikal: Stupid Somers is rolingk outside! Somers gets to the outside and begins rummaging around under the ring. The announcers query this turn of events as it is a "“No Gimmick” Gimmick Match. Chase: You really have to query Mal’s intentions here as this is a "“No Gimmick” Gimmick Match. Right. Eventually Somers pulls out a square white box from under the ring and then goes to Gary Trudeau. They confer a little as Somers gets back into the ring. He opens the box and pulls out… Chase: TWISTER!! Mal has Twister! Mikal: Ah Mikal love that game, especially naked at Russian nursing home. Chase: Whatever, I don’t think Mal can be disqualified as he hasn’t hit Dave with the plastic sheet. The crowd cheers as Somers lays the mat out and drags Harley over to it. He motions to Trudeau. Gary Trudeau: Ladies and Gentlemen, welcome to a brief game of Twister, which is literally scheduled for the first fall. Gary spins the little arm around on the board Mal gave him. Gary Trudeau: Mal, left foot red! Somers puts his left foot across the prone Harley. Gary Trudeau: Dave, right foot blue! Harley is still a bit woozy so Mal grabs Daves leg and pulls it across him. Gary Trudeau: Mal, right foot yellow! Somers grins as he grabs Harley and spins around on the Twister mat. Chase: SHARPSHOOTER! Sharpshooter by Mal, all thanks to Twister! Gary resumes his seat as Dave is awoken by the hold. He struggles to escape as Mal torques in the hold, some of the crowd booing, some of the crowd cheering. Sweat pours off both men as they jockey for position. Chase: Dave is using his strength to push up off the mat, but Mal has that hold locked in tight. Suddenly, the combination of sweat on the plastic Twister mat is too much and both men slip over, crumbling to the canvas in a twist of human wreckage, as the crowd cheers. The referee picks up the mat and throws it to the outside to some lucky, and now sweat drenched fan, as both men slowly rise. Chase: Someone gets a souvenir tonight. Mikal: I’m hoping for Brooke’s number. Chase: In your dreams. Standing ten count. 1! 2! 3! 4! 5! As Somers and Harley try to gain their feet, we can see the fan fight for the twister mat. Sadly it is ripped to pieces for its every fans dream to get some sweat off X-corp. Well every mentally disturbed fans dream. 6! 7! Chase: Somers and Harley are on their feet. Mikal: I vas hoping for ten count to end this. Chase: Both men tie up. Somers whips Harley into the ropes. As Harley rebounds, he leaps at Mal with a huge clothesline. But Mal ducks and Harley falls flat on his face. Capitalising, Somers puts Harley in an ankle lock, but before he can apply adequate pressure Harley crawls to the ropes and pulls himself to his feet. But Mal grabs Harley around the waist and goes for a belly to back suplex. Chase: Somers not letting up with this offence. Mikal: Harley is weak. Harley grabs hold of the ropes just as Mal is about to pick him up. He elbows Mal in the ribs, causing him to stagger backwards. As he does, Harley climbs out on to the apron and springboards back in, taking Mal down with a cross body. Chase: Look at that! Harley finds his opening! Mikal: And pin. 1! 2! Chase: Easy kickout by Mal. Dave scoops Somers off the mat and bodyslams him to the canvas. Harley gets to his feet and scales the ropes. Chase: Monkey is getting high! Mikal: Vhere is animal welfare when you need them? Unfortunately it takes Dave a little too long to get up top and Somers kips up, scaling the same corner ropes and bringing Dave down with a surprise armdrag from up top. The crowd applauds in response. Somers squats near Harley, hooking his legs around his opponent, then grabbing his arms, then rolling onto his back, holding Harley above him. Chase: LA MAJISTRAL by Somers, a hold not often seen in the WWA. Mikal: Vhat strength! What strength indeed, as after about 3 seconds, Dave’s weight is too much and Somers breaks the hold, sending Harley crashing onto him. The referee has no choice but to count. 1! 2! Chase: Kickout in the nick of time by a squashed Somers. Mikal: Malcolm in the middle! Of Monkey and Mat! Chase: Only Mal Somers could do that. Mal Pushes Dave off. Both take some time to get to their feet and tie up again. Mal quickly has Dave in a side headlock. Dave pushes Mal off him and in to the ropes. As he bounces back, Dave hits him with the biggest monkey flip you have ever seen. Chase: Mad Monkey goes Ape with a Monkey Flip!!! Mikal: Bah. Too many monkey jokes. Mal sits up and holds his back. Dave just spins around and kicks him in the face. He then grabs Mal’s leg, bends it back and sits down. Chase: Harley back on that leg. He applies an inside toe hold. Harley releases and assists Mal to his feet Harley quickly elbows Mal in the gut and sliding behind him. He sets him up and applies an abdominal stretch. Chase: Harley with an abdominal stretch. He tightens the hold and looks to the crowd for approval. They cheer him on. He releases the hold, raising Mal’s arms and taking him down with a full nelson slam. Mal crawls around the ring clutching his back as Dave smiles to the cheering crowd. Chase: Harley looks in control. Mikal: That is scary. Dave looks at the corner and then at Mal. He can’t help smiling and goes for the ropes. Scaling to the top rope. He goes to leap and Mal has enough in the tank to lift himself off the mat. Chase: Missile dropkick! Mikal: By Somers on the rope! Mal Leaps hitting the top rope. Sending Dave crashing to the canvass. A continuing theme occurs in this match as both men lie on the canvass. Both looking spent, but Mal Somers has something left. As he lifts himself off the mat. Chase: Mal is up. Mikal: And going up. Dave struggles to get to his feet as Mal ascends the turnbuckle. Chase: Mal is heading up top. Could he be going for a Somers Assault? Mikal: Somers IS assault…on eyes! Hahahaha! The crowd cheer as Mal stands ready on the top turnbuckle. The cheers of the crowd are almost deafening… Crowd: SOMERS! HARLEY! SOMERS! HARLEY! Well actually, mixed together the crowd sound more like… Crowd: HARLERS! HARLERS! HARLERS! Mal stands soaking in the cheers. He raises his head to the sky in a salute to the dearly departed X Corp mascot. Chase: Dave is up! Look out Mal! Dave dives at the turnbuckle and throws a few punches, knocking Mal down to a seated position on top of the turnbuckle. Chase: Both men fighting on the turnbuckle. How will this end?! Mikal: Vith Mikal beating all of puny X Corp. Dave climbs around behind Mal and manages to turn him around, setting him up for… Chase: Dave’s set up for a top rope Harley Bomb!!!
